2026-11-03T19:45:44.7545396Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 30 with the QKV-only tail split prewarmed before readiness

Round 29 adopted the QKV-only split, but its score fell to 9777.16. The later analysis found that the steady Prefill supply rate was unchanged while the measured-phase burst started about 1.49 s late. Every serving process logged its first split dispatch only after the measured phase began, because M=7988 first appears there: warmup inputs are mostly 3989 tokens and measured inputs 3986. The gate runs in separate processes with their own DeepGEMM cache, so the first JIT or load of the 52-row tail kernel most likely landed at the head of the measured burst.

Round 30 runs the body and tail GEMMs once in each Prefill target process after weight processing and before KV pools, CUDA graphs and readiness. It uses the serving shapes, dtypes, scale stride and call arguments. The rules allow engine preparation before the official command. The gate, image a001f901 and the post-greedy budget are unchanged. The prewarm time and serving dispatch lines are saved to results/prefill-r30-dispatch-0..2.txt.

Success means at most 111 to 113 measured-burst TTFT misses, a first-token intercept near 0.76 s and a score above the main range of 10020 to 10025. If the score only matches main, the tail split line ends.

This commit only adds an sbatch comment so the push passes the workflow path filter.
